"https://jmp2.uk/stvp-USBA3800004EL", "clearkey": null, "headers": {} } ]; let player = null; function log(msg) { document.getElementById('log').innerText = "Status: " + msg; } async function initApp() { const video = document.getElementById('video'); shaka.polyfill.installAll(); if (!shaka.Player.isBrowserSupported()) { log('Browser not supported for Shaka Player!'); return; } player = new shaka.Player(video); player.addEventListener('error', (event) => { console.error('Shaka Error:', event.detail); log('Error Code ' + event.detail.code + ' - ' + event.detail.message); }); renderPlaylist(); if (channels.length > 0) { loadChannel(0); } } function renderPlaylist() { const listDiv = document.getElementById('playlist'); listDiv.innerHTML = ''; channels.forEach((ch, index) => { const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.innerText = ch.name; btn.onclick = () => loadChannel(index); btn.id = 'btn-' + index; listDiv.appendChild(btn); }); } async function loadChannel(index) { document.querySelectorAll('.playlist button').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active')); document.getElementById('btn-' + index).classList.add('active'); const ch = channels[index]; log('Loading ' + ch.name + '...'); // Reset player configuration player.configure({ drm: { clearKeys: ch.clearkey ? ch.clearkey : {} } }); // Configure headers if present player.getNetworkingEngine().clearAllRequestFilters(); if (ch.headers && Object.keys(ch.headers).length > 0) { player.getNetworkingEngine().registerRequestFilter((type, request) => { if (type === shaka.net.NetworkingEngine.RequestType.MANIFEST || type === shaka.net.NetworkingEngine.RequestType.SEGMENT) { for (let [key, value] of Object.entries(ch.headers)) { request.headers[key] = value; } } }); } try { await player.load(ch.url); log('Playing: ' + ch.name); } catch (e) { log('Failed to load channel (Code: ' + e.code + ')'); } } document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initApp);
